- Pad: frontend controls (FrontendUp/Down/Left/Right, LB/RB, Accept, Cancel, X held for x10) only while `IS_USING_KEYBOARD_AND_MOUSE` is false, so keys never count twice. No mouse.
- Safety: enRAGE pages only when `EnrageHidesKnown` and `CanReloadEnrage` (else a message page; its bookkeeping like Seeded/Cleared/*Day must never become editable). Left/Right changes are staged and written after 450 ms without change, or on moving on or closing (`Flush`). If the "settings panel" feature switches itself off, `PanelOpen` is false and the panel is closed so keys and the ticker come back.
- A new key setting goes in `SettingsPanel.OurKeys` (so the panel sets it by key press) and in `HeldKeyRepeat` (one action per press). `TickerNextKey` (T, 0.6.0): `Ticker.NextPart` jumps a line shown in parts to its next part, else `TickerBrain.Skip()` moves on (from a thought to the news).

## An execution on the road (0.6.3, Lex 2026-10-26)

- After the game's own `re_executions` (two Lemoyne Raiders about to shoot Zeke Townsend; enRAGE named and remembered him). The game's encounter scripts own their peds and can't be started, so this is our own, built like the crying woman. `Core/Executions.cs` (`Plan`: Reason debt / feud / informer / witness from `InnerLife.WorryNow` (debt, feud, law|secret, anything else); Boss from `WorryFill("{who}")`, the same name his thoughts use, and the eleven dollars; Captors: boss (the creditor himself when the worry's `WorryLink.OtherId` is a free male regular with a look, plus a hired man), hired, kin, outlaws; `GangOf(region)`: raiders (Lemoyne towns), murfrees (Roanoke), odriscolls; `GangModels` only models enRAGE spawns: g_m_y_uniexconfeds_01/02 and g_m_o_uniexconfeds_01, g_m_m_unimountainmen_01, g_m_m_uniduster_01-03; all lines: Threat, Plead, WarnYou, CallsToYou, LastWords, Done, Thanks, During, After, Found). `Core/Engine.Executions.cs`: `ExecutionPlan`, `ExecutionSeen` (Sightings 2, so the corner's met rule lets his news through), `Rescued` (Talks, Affection +30, `rescued:<day>` and `rescue reason:` marks, a `rescue` good deed with its own lines), `Executed` (`QueueDeathNews`, and a `LaterAct` "news" for the corner when he's found, 6-18 h), `AfterRescue` (45% of `ResidentThought` for 3 days).
- `Game/WestworldScript.Executions.cs`: rolled in `EncountersTick` (05:00-22:00, `TownOnly` null, `DoneToday("execution")`); `ExecutionCaptive` (male regulars with Model, not bound, dead, fled or a keeper; the nearest town's first, worry-reasoned first); spawned 45-70 m off out of sight (`EncounterSpot`), kneeling (`WORLD_HUMAN_GRAVE_MOURNING_KNEEL`), dressed in his preset and bound in `_resBind` (so the ledger notes his death with its cause); the men 2.6 m behind with revolvers and `TASK_AIM_GUN_AT_ENTITY`, in `_encounterPeds` and `_cameAtYouSeen` (renewed every step: the entry lasts 5 min); a boss regular dressed and bound too. A `WhiteDot` blip. Noticed (within 40 m, or 75 m in sight): `ExecutionSeen`, then 40 s (25 s once warned at 28 m), or at once when he rides past 110 m; he calls to you at 60 m (subtitled), the first man warns you (subtitled, naming who it's between), his `During` thought on the ticker, overheard threats and pleas by turns. Engaged (a man hurt or killed by you, 1.2 s of free aim at one, or within 6 m): `MakeHostile` + combat; saved when none stand within 70 m of him (`Engine.Rescued`, his thanks). Else (25 s after he's noticed, 12 s once warned, with `Executions.LastChance` said to him 6 s before) last words; the captive's kneel is cleared and his events unblocked so he tries to get up, then the first man is set on him with `TASK_COMBAT_PED` (`firing`, the game's own shot: Lex's second test, 22:02, saw no shot and a loop with the old bullet-from-nowhere); still alive 3 s on: `ExecutionBullet` (`SHOOT_SINGLE_BULLET_BETWEEN_COORDS`, health 0, ragdoll); `ExecutionDead`: `LedgerNote(..., "another")`, `Engine.Executed`, they walk off. Never noticed in 4 min (8 for the test): ends quietly; gone from the world before he's seen to die: ends quietly (no `Executed`); died after he was saved: the ledger's only; the fight ends 3 min on at most (`EncounterLive` would block every other encounter). His kin's news is `QueueDeathNews(byYou: false)` (`Rumor.NotByYou`): `DeliverDeath` gives the telegram and plain grief, never an avenger or a grief letter to the player (the path was built for the player's own kills). The news names the town nearest where it happened. `EndExecution` deletes only the unseen living that enRAGE hasn't looked at (`Bridge.Looked`). Kept out of street scenes (`IsExecutionPed` in Reasons.cs) and on the ticker through `ExecutionNear`. Test: `[` (`ExecutionKey`, settings version 62; enRAGE uses no [) or the Testing page's "Stage an execution nearby" (`stage-execution`), both setting `_execRequested` through `RequestEncounter` (see "Night encounters"); F7's encounters line. Offline: ticktest `ExecutionsTest`. Seen in game 2026-10-26: Lex saved the captive in the first test; after the shot fix (`6de35d2`) the execution itself worked (Lex, later the same night).
## Strangers at a campfire (night 2026-10-28, item 14; OVERNIGHT.md Tier 4's 3)

- `Core/Campfire.cs`: the hours (20:00-04:00), the hail, the welcome, five tales of four lines with the others' answers, the farewell, the mutter, the plea at a gun, the traveller made room for; `JoinerChance` 40. Game (`Game/WestworldScript.Campfire.cs`, in the encounter framework like the traveller: the roll's eighth kind, `EncounterLive`, the tick, `EndEncounters`, F7 `TEST campfire:`, the Testing row `campfire` (a test takes turns with and without the traveller), `[Encounters] Campfires` (on), `CampfireChance` (15), settings version 90, Pacing "campfire" (15%, ten game hours between)): out of the towns at night, `CampfireSpawn` lights a fire 40-70 m off out of sight (`EncounterSpot`, the campfire object as the hermit's) with two or three men sat round it (`WORLD_CAMP_FIRE_SIT_GROUND`, events blocked, no weapons); within 25 m in view the host calls you over (said to you); on foot by the fire 3 s he welcomes you and tells the tale a line every 6 s, the second man answering; then the farewell; past 60 m after the hail a mutter; up from the fire past 12 m the farewell; a gun on him ends it; once you have sat (20 s or more after the fire was lit) and are within 40 m, a traveller walks in from the dark and sits (`CampfireJoiner`), the host makes room. Over at 80 m or two minutes: the men deleted unseen and let go in sight, the fire deleted unseen; `encounter:campfire` recorded. Not yet seen in game: the sit scenario round a placed fire, the joiner's walk and seat.

## The voice below the floor (night 2026-10-28, item 14; OVERNIGHT.md Tier 4's 6; West World)

- `Core/Below.cs`: the hours (23:00-04:00), the barkeep's thoughts as it knocks (escalating: the pipes, "he'll quiet down by morning", "I know that voice"), his answer if you put it to him, his thought once it stops, the town's word next morning (`Engine.BelowHeard`, a news `LaterAct` 07:00-09:00). Game (`Game/WestworldScript.Below.cs`, `BelowStep` from Tick04 after the grudges, `Safe("voice below")`, `[Encounters] VoiceBelow` on with West World, settings version 90, Pacing "below" 25% once a night a town, the Testing row `below`, F7 `TEST voice below:`): at night in a saloon with the town's barkeep within 14 m (`PostKindOf`, `HolderAt`), a ped spawned 2.6 m under the boards behind the bar (invisible, no collision, frozen, invincible) pleads (`Speech.Plead`) every 15-25 s while you're within 10 m, four times at most, the keeper's thought over his head each time; stand over the boards 1.5 s and it stops; stand before the keeper 2.5 s and he answers you; over at 25 m or four minutes, the unseen one deleted. Nothing is found, nobody harmed. Not yet seen in game: whether the plea carries through the floor from an invisible ped.

## The doctor's patient (night 2026-10-28, item 14; OVERNIGHT.md Tier 4's 16)

- `Core/Patient.cs`: the doctor's hours (08:00-18:00), `Takes` (the fever worry at stages 1 and 2), the thoughts going, inside and coming home by stage. Game (`Game/WestworldScript.Patient.cs`, `PatientScene` from the drama tick after the lost child, `PatientStep` from its top; `[Scenes] DoctorsPatient` on, settings version 90; Pacing "patient" 20%, twelve game hours between, a day a person; the Testing row `patient`; F7 `TEST doctor's patient:`): by day in a town with its doctor at his post within 90 m, a reason candidate whose own worry is the fever at stage 1 or 2, free and in view within 16 m (`ReasonRange`), walks to the doctor's position (two minutes at most), stands 20 s, walks back, a thought over their head at each turn (`_ticker.Push`), then wanders (`WanderLater`); `NoteInScene` keeps the other features off them. Not yet seen in game: whether the nav mesh takes a walker to the doctor's door inside his shop. Not built: the horse thief at the hitching post (the repo knows no hitching posts or hitched horses).


## Night encounters (0.6.0)

- The Testing page since the overnight run (2026-10-27, settings version 66): `Core/TestActions.cs` is the catalogue (id, group, label, key setting, default key, needs; `Field` when the key is one of Settings' own fields: StalkerKey, WakeKey, ExecutionKey, CryingWomanKey, HermitKey, GatheringKey; the rest in `Settings.TestKeyMap`, read by `Load` for any catalogue key that isn't a field, written by `ToIni` under `[Testing]` with `TestKeys` (master) and `ShowMinds`; `TestKeyOf(entry)`, `ValueText` knows them). The panel builds the page from the catalogue by group (`Row.Fire`: since 2026-10-27, Lex, Enter captures its key as on every key row and Space runs `fire:<id>` and closes the panel (`FireNow`; a gamepad's A, sent as Enter, fires too, and Space while waiting for a key fires); Left/Right only say how; Delete writes None. A key another of ours has moves over: `OurKeyHolder` (a blank Testing key counts as its action's default, `KeyNow`), cleared to None in the same save if `Clearable` (Testing keys, SettingsKey2), else refused; enRAGE's keys (`EnrageKeyOwner`) are always refused. Before: Enter ran it and Right twice captured) and skips the `[Testing]` section as an ordinary page; `IsOurKey`/`IsTestKey`/`Clearable` replaced the fixed key lists; `KeyOwner` refuses a key another RealNPCs key or enRAGE's `[Keys]` already has (compared through `KeySpec.Parse(...).ToString()`), naming the owner. `Game/WestworldScript.Testing.cs`: `BuildTestKeys` (ApplySettings), `TestKeyHit` (KeyDown04, before the wake/ticker keys) and the panel's `PanelAction("fire:<id>")` only ask (`AskFire`, the panel's a second later); `TestingStep` (Tick04 before the mission check, `Safe("testing")`) fires it once the player has control (waits up to 10 s), `TestRail` (engine loaded, no story mission, alive, control) then `TestDispatch` to the feature's own path; logs `TEST fire <id>: started (how; skipped: ...)` / `refused: <why>`; F7 `TEST testing:`. The hard-wired Shift+F7/F9/F2 KeySpecs are gone (TestTalkKey, TestRoughKey, TestFontKey). Street scenes on demand (settings version 67): `scene:<kind>` rows go to `FireScene`, which takes `ReasonCandidates(me, now, test: true)` (rests skipped), tries the eight nearest in pairs (both orders, at most 10 m apart; `PairWont`: two men for brawl, rough, drunk, cheat, standoff; a man thief for pickpocket; a man and a woman for flirt, propose, court, x:sweet, x:courting unless SameSexRomance), `BuildCast`, `TroubleCant` (a setting that is off refuses at once), then `BuildSceneFor` and `AddMeet`, the same two functions `ReasonScene` now calls (the per-kind building moved out of it unchanged), and `StartScene(..., test: true)`: no tie, memory or SawScene. `court` turns its answer yes, maybe, no per fire (`_courtTurn`). Other rows: `plea` arms the next aim within a minute (`_pleaArmedUntil`: no 25 s wait, anyone, 300 ms of aim), `arrival` forces `QueueArrival` (now `bool`, `force` skips the roll), `letter` hands over waiting letters (or writes a test thanks letter from the person talked to most, titled "(a test)"), `musing` (`StrangerMusing` now `bool`), `glitch`, `disbelief` (the nearest named person within 15 m). `replay` fires the last id with its seed (`_lastFireSeed`). Wake stages over heads (2026-10-27, Lex; `[Testing] ShowWakeStages`, settings version 76, a Switches row and the Tools row `wakestages`; `Game/WestworldScript.WakeTags.cs`, `Safe("wake stages")` from `Frame` after Show minds): people within 25 m on screen whom `WakeIdentity(p, false, ...)` knows at stage 1+, at most eight, nearest first, rebuilt every 500 ms: one line "Stirring (1 of 4)" .. "Awake (4 of 4)" (pale, amber, orange, red) plus what holds them back (keeps a counter, had their chance (`despair:rolled2`), riding, can't be directed now), anchored at `ThoughtAboveHead`; a thought over the same person is lifted by `WakeTagLift`; log `SHOW wake stages:` once; F7 `TEST wake stages:`. Show minds (`Game/WestworldScript.ShowMinds.cs`, `Safe("show minds")` from `Frame`; on as it comes since settings version 77, Lex 2026-10-27, an ini's old "off" raised once in `RaiseOldDefaults`; Ctrl+F2 turns it off and on for the session since version 83; the card sits clear above the thought over the same head, in screen space: room for two of the thought's lines over the wake tag always, so it holds still as thoughts come and go, and above a longer thought while one shows (`_overHeadTopY`, recorded as the thought box is drawn; Lex's screenshot, 2026-10-27, had its last line on a two-line thought)): up to four people the add-on already knows within 11 m (`MindCardMetres`; 15 until Lex's call, 2026-10-27) (`KnownPerson`: a record, an acquaintance, a bound resident; nobody is made up), their card from `Engine.MindCard` (Core: name, temper, home; mood and worry status; ties and memories, the latest; the strongest candidate's source and pull, nothing worn), rebuilt every 500 ms, drawn each frame 1 m over the head bone, blue-edged; log `SHOW minds: the first card`; F7 `TEST show minds:`. Before that (0.6.3, Lex: "a Testing page ... a hotkey to spawn in the NPC encounters"; settings version 63): the panel's Testing page (after Hotkeys) holds `TestingKeys` (StalkerKey `]`, ExecutionKey `[`, and `CryingWomanKey`, `HermitKey`, `GatheringKey`, `None` as they come; all in `OurKeys`, `HeldKeyRepeat`, kept off the Hotkeys page; `Clearable`: Delete while setting one writes `None`) and action rows for the same (`stage-execution|crying|hermit|gathering`) plus the memory wipe. A key or row calls `RequestEncounter(kind, how, out asked)`: refused while the kind (or the master, or West World for the awake) is off or any encounter is live or asked for, else `_execRequested` / `_encounterRequested`; `EncountersTick` takes it once nothing is live and `EncounterBlocker` is clear (else a `Not now:` hint) and calls `StageEncounter`: any hour and anywhere (a hermit's camp, which is saved, still never in a town and within `MaxCamps`), never marking the night done (`Crying.Test`, `Gathering.Test`, `Execution.Test`, `NewCamp(now, test)`). Log `ENCOUNTER test, kind (key X | the settings panel): staging one near you`.
- `[Encounters]` with its own master (`Encounters`); `Gated()` turns all kinds off with it, and the hermits and gatherings (awake people) off with `WestWorld` too. Decisions and lines in `Core/Encounters.cs`, the game side in `Game/WestworldScript.Encounters.cs` (`EncountersStep` in `Tick04`, `EndEncounters` in `MissionPause` and `Shutdown04`).
- One roll per kind every `EncounterEveryMinutes`, gated by `EncounterBlocker` (StalkBlocker plus stalker, scenes, wedding, plea, theft); `StalkBlocker` returns "a night encounter is going on" while one runs. Once a night per kind (`NightIndex`: noon to noon, in `_store.Counts["enc:<kind>"]`).
- The crying woman (built): hidden spot via `EncounterSpot` (BehindCamera + StandSpot, not visible), kneel scenario, help lines, then knife / ambush / dark. Attackers go into `REALNPCS_HOSTILE` (hostile both ways with the player's group) and `_cameAtYouSeen`, so Watch.cs counts it as self-defence. The dead are never deleted.
- The hermit (built): `Engine.Flee` at the WakeActs escape arrival (stage 4 residents), `Resident.Fled`/`HasCamp`/`CampX..Z`; ResidentOf never recasts the fled. `Game/WestworldScript.Hermits.cs`: camps (made 60-140 m off) spawn their hermit within 150 m when out of view, gone past 200 m, behaviour by `Encounters.HermitNext`. Not bound in `_resBind` (so wake acts and the wake key leave them alone); RefreshNear has an explicit `IsHermit` path; OnKilled lets a hermit's death through. `EncounterLive` includes a hermit out, so stalkers and other encounters wait near a camp.
- The gathering (built): `Game/WestworldScript.Gatherings.cs`, geometry and creep in `Core/Encounters.cs` (`CirclePoint`, `Heading`, `CreepStep`). Same rules as the hermit for the ticker (`IsGatherer` in RefreshNear) and kills. The trigger-line spreading (awake people waking others) is not part of it.
- Not yet seen in game.
